Note for my Ubuntu VM, don't forget to use:
    source /opt/ros/jazzy/setup.bash

Setup for Ubuntu 24.04.3 LTS, Jazzy (Works for Windows 10 too):
    Terminal commands:

        Block 1:
            locale  # check for UTF-8

            sudo apt update && sudo apt install locales
            sudo locale-gen en_US en_US.UTF-8
            sudo update-locale LC_ALL=en_US.UTF-8 LANG=en_US.UTF-8
            export LANG=en_US.UTF-8

            locale  # verify settings

        Block 2:
            sudo apt install software-properties-common
            sudo add-apt-repository universe

        Block 3:
            sudo apt update && sudo apt install curl -y
            export ROS_APT_SOURCE_VERSION=$(curl -s https://api.github.com/repos/ros-infrastructure/ros-apt-source/releases/latest | grep -F "tag_name" | awk -F\" '{print $4}')
            curl -L -o /tmp/ros2-apt-source.deb "https://github.com/ros-infrastructure/ros-apt-source/releases/download/${ROS_APT_SOURCE_VERSION}/ros2-apt-source_${ROS_APT_SOURCE_VERSION}.$(. /etc/os-release && echo ${UBUNTU_CODENAME:-${VERSION_CODENAME}})_all.deb"
            sudo dpkg -i /tmp/ros2-apt-source.deb


        4: 
            sudo apt update

        5:
            sudo apt upgrade

        6:
            sudo apt install ros-jazzy-desktop

        7:
            sudo apt update
            sudo apt install ros-jazzy-navigation2 ros-jazzy-nav2-bringup

        source /opt/ros/jazzy/setup.bash


    Terminal commands to run:
        For my Ubuntu VM:
            Terminal 1:
            source /opt/ros/jazzy/setup.bash
            cd /MRTP/MRTP/
            colcon build
            source install/setup.bash
            ros2 launch gazeboenvs tb4_warehouse.launch.py use_rviz:=true

            Terminal 2:
            source /opt/ros/jazzy/setup.bash
            cd /MRTP/MRTP/
            source install/setup.bash
            cd ../tb4_humans_ws/
            colcon build
            source install/setup.bash
            ros2 run tb4_human_detector human_detector_node 



This is everything I have right now. I'd like to try autonomous coverage algorithm 
    because I want the robot to use the map's data and generate its own path to cover 
    all the free space and check if each human as moved and where their new 
    coordinates are if they have moved.


My Note:
    How I generated map details:
        ethan@Mac docker % make shell
        docker exec -it ae507374fa1a bash
        (.venv) root@docker-desktop:/MRTP# cd MRTP/
        (.venv) root@docker-desktop:/MRTP/MRTP# source install/setup.bash 
        (.venv) root@docker-desktop:/MRTP/MRTP# ros2 run nav2_map_server map_saver_cli -f ~/my_warehouse_map
        (.venv) root@docker-desktop:/MRTP/MRTP# cd /root
        (.venv) root@docker-desktop:~# ls -la my_warehouse_map.*
        (.venv) root@docker-desktop:~# cp my_warehouse_map.* /MRTP/MRTP/
